  r6 'Aiming at the pain points of information fragmentation, lack of audit process, unrecorded claim and lack of dishonesty control when lost items in colleges are spread through class groups and offline bulletin boards, this paper designs and implements a campus lost and found management system based on SpringBoot and Vue. The system adopts front-end and back-end separation architecture, the back-end integrates MyBatis-Plus and Redis cache, and the front-end is developed with Vue3 and Element Plus, distinguishing two roles: ordinary students and campus administrators. Students can publish lost and found information online, leave messages for consultation and initiate item claims; administrators are responsible for information review, dishonesty blacklist management, campus announcement release and visual statistics of lost data. The system realizes a full business closed loop of information release, review, message, claim, dishonesty control and data statistics, simplifies the management process of lost items on campus, and improves the matching efficiency of teachers and students looking for lost items. The test results show that the system has stable response and complete functions, which can meet the daily lost management needs of colleges.' kinds=['rPr', 't']
